<div className="bt">
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 3:38; Genesis 4:25–32"> Adam (Man, ground; red; earthling), Shet &#91;Compensation&#93;, Enosh &#91;man&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> Keinan &#91;possession&#93;, Mahalal’el &#91;praise of God&#93;, Yered &#91;descent&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Judah 1:14; Genesis 5:21–31; Luke 3:36–37"> Henokh &#91;dedicated&#93;, Metushelach &#91;man of the dart&#93;, Lemekh (poor; made low),</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Genesis 5:32; Genesis 9:18; Luke 17:26"> No’ah &#91;Motion&#93;, Shem &#91;name&#93;, Ham &#91;hot&#93;, and Yefet &#91;opened&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 10:1–5; Ezekiel 38:6; Ezekiel 27:13"> The sons of Yefet &#91;opened&#93;; Gomer &#91;complete&#93;, and Magog &#91;land of God&#93;, and Madai &#91;middle land&#93;, and Yavan (Greece) &#91;Ionia&#93;, and Tuval &#91;you shall be brought&#93;, and Meshekh &#91;drawing out&#93;, and Tiras &#91;desire&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 10:3"> And the sons of Gomer &#91;complete&#93;; Ashchenaz &#91;a man as sprinkled: fire as scattered&#93;, and Rifat &#91;spoken&#93;, and Togarmah &#91;you will break her&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Jeremiah 2:10; Numbers 24:24; Isaiah 66:19"> And the sons of Yavan (Greece) &#91;Ionia&#93;; Elishah &#91;God of the coming one&#93;, and Tarshish &#91;yellow jasper&#93;, Kittim &#91;bruisers&#93;, and Dodanim &#91;leaders&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> The sons of Ham &#91;hot&#93;; Kush &#91;black&#93; (Ethiopia) &#91;black&#93;, and Mitzrayim &#91;land of the Copts&#93;, Put &#91;a bow&#93;, and Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> And the sons of Kush &#91;black&#93; (Ethiopia) &#91;black&#93;; S’va &#91;drink you&#93;, and Havilah (Stretch of Sand), and Savta &#91;striking&#93;, and Ra’mah &#91;horse’s mane&#93;, and Savt’kha &#91;striking&#93;. And the sons of Ra’mah &#91;horse’s mane&#93;; Sheva &#91;Seven&#93;, and D’dan &#91;low country&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Genesis 10:8–12; Micah 5:6"> And Kush &#91;black&#93; (Ethiopia) &#91;black&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Nimrod &#91;rebellion, or the valiant&#93;: he began to be mighty upon the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 10:13–18"> And Mitzrayim &#91;land of the Copts&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Ludim &#91;to the firebrands: travailings&#93;, and ‘Anamim &#91;affliction of the waters&#93;, and L’havim &#91;flames&#93;, and Naftuchim &#91;openings&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Deuteronomy 2:23; Jeremiah 47:4; Amos 9:7"> And Patrusim &#91;region of the south (Pathros)&#93;, and Kasluchim &#91;fortified&#93;, (of whom came the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;,) and Kaftorim &#91;a crown (Caphtor)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Genesis 9:22; Genesis 23:20; Genesis 10:15–19"> And Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Tzidon &#91;Hunting&#93; his firstborn, and Het &#91;terror&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Genesis 15:21; Joshua 3:10; Exodus 33:2"> The Y’vusi &#91;Threshers&#93; also, and the Emori &#91;Rebellious Highlanders&#93;, and the Girgashi &#91;dwelling on a clayey soil&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Exodus 3:8; 1 Kings 9:20; Exodus 13:5"> And the Hivi &#91;Wicked Villagers&#93;, and the ‘Arki &#91;gnawing&#93;, and the Sini &#91;thorn, or clay&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 34:8; 1 Kings 8:65"> And the Arvadi &#91;I shall break loose&#93;, and the Tz’mari &#91;double woolens&#93;, and the Hamati &#91;fortress&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Genesis 10:22–32; Ezra 4:2; Ezekiel 32:24"> The sons of Shem &#91;name&#93;; ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93;, and Ashur &#91;A Level Plain&#93;, and Arpakhshad &#91;I shall fail as the breast: he cursed the breast-bottle&#93;, and Lud &#91;strife&#93;, and Aram &#91;exalted&#93;, and ‘Utz &#91;wooded&#93;, and Hul &#91;circle&#93;, and Geter &#91;fear&#93;, and Meshekh &#91;drawing out&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Genesis 11:12–15; Genesis 10:24"> And Arpakhshad &#91;I shall fail as the breast: he cursed the breast-bottle&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Shelah &#91;A Petition&#93;, and Shelah &#91;A Petition&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) ‘Ever &#91;The Region Beyond&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Genesis 10:21; Genesis 11:16–17; Genesis 10:25"> And unto ‘Ever &#91;The Region Beyond&#93; were born two sons: the name of the one &#91;was&#93; Peleg &#91;division&#93;; because in his days the earth was divided: and his brother’s name &#91;was&#93; Yoktan &#91;smallness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 10:26–27"> And Yoktan &#91;smallness&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Almodad &#91;not measured&#93;, and Shelef &#91;a drawing forth&#93;, and Hatzar-Mavet &#91;village of death&#93;, and Yerach &#91;new moon&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Hadoram &#91;noble honour&#93; also, and Uzal &#91;I shall be flooded&#93;, and Diklah &#91;palm grove&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Genesis 10:28"> And ‘Eival &#91;Stone&#93;, and Avima’el &#91;my father is El (God)&#93;, and Sheva &#91;Seven&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="1 Kings 10:11; Genesis 25:18; 1 Samuel 15:7"> And Ofir &#91;reducing to ashes&#93;, and Havilah (Stretch of Sand), and Yovav &#91;A Desert&#93;. All these &#91;were&#93; the sons of Yoktan &#91;smallness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Genesis 11:10–26; Luke 3:34–36"> Shem &#91;name&#93;, Arpakhshad &#91;I shall fail as the breast: he cursed the breast-bottle&#93;, Shelah &#91;A Petition&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Luke 3:35"> ‘Ever &#91;The Region Beyond&#93;, Peleg &#91;division&#93;, Re’u &#91;friend&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Luke 3:34–35"> S’rug &#91;branch&#93;, Nachor &#91;snorting&#93;, Terach &#91;station&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Genesis 11:27–32; Nehemiah 9:7; Joshua 24:2"> Avram &#91;exalted father&#93;; the same &#91;is&#93; Avraham &#91;Exalted Father of a Multitude&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 17:19–21; Genesis 21:2–5; Genesis 21:9–10"> The sons of Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;; Yitz’khak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and Yishma’el &#91;God will hear&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Isaiah 60:7; Genesis 25:12–16; Song of Solomon 1:5"> These &#91;are&#93; their generations: The firstborn of Yishma’el &#91;God will hear&#93;, N’vayot &#91;heights&#93;; then Kedar &#91;dark&#93;, and Adbe’el &#91;chastened of God&#93;, and Mivsam &#91;sweet odour&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Isaiah 21:11; Genesis 25:15"> Mishma &#91;a hearing&#93;, and Dumah &#91;Silence&#93;, Massa, Hadad &#91;mighty&#93;, and Teima &#91;desert&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Y’turn &#91;enclosed&#93;, Nafish &#91;refreshment&#93;, and Kedemah &#91;original&#93;. These are the sons of Yishma’el &#91;God will hear&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Genesis 25:1–4; Jeremiah 25:23; Judges 6:1–6"> Now the sons of K’turah &#91;incense&#93;, Avraham’s &#91;Exalted Father&#93; concubine: she bore Zimran &#91;musician&#93;, and Yokshan &#91;snarer&#93;, and Medan &#91;strife&#93;, and Midyan &#91;Strife&#93;, and Yishbak &#91;he releases&#93;, and Shuach &#91;wealth&#93;. And the sons of Yokshan &#91;snarer&#93;; Sheva &#91;Seven&#93;, and D’dan &#91;low country&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Isaiah 60:6"> And the sons of Midyan &#91;Strife&#93;; ‘Eifah &#91;gloomy&#93;, and ‘Efer &#91;a calf&#93;, and Henokh &#91;dedicated&#93;, and Avida &#91;my father knows&#93;, and Elda’ah &#91;God has known&#93;. All these &#91;are&#93; the sons of K’turah &#91;incense&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Genesis 32:28; Acts 7:8; Gosel 1:2"> And Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Yitz’khak &#91;Laughter&#93;. The sons of Yitz’khak &#91;Laughter&#93;; ‘Esav &#91;Hairy&#93; and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Genesis 36:9–10; Genesis 36:4–5"> The sons of ‘Esav &#91;Hairy&#93;; Elifaz &#91;my God is (fine) gold&#93;, Re’u &#91;friend&#93;’el &#91;friend of God&#93;, and Ye’ush &#91;He hurries to aid&#93;, and Ya’lam &#91;concealed&#93;, and Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Jeremiah 49:20; Amos 1:12; Obadiah 1:9"> The sons of Elifaz &#91;my God is (fine) gold&#93;; Teman &#91;south&#93;, and Omar &#91;speaker, or eloquent&#93;, Tzefi &#91;watchtower&#93;, and Ga’tam &#91;a burnt valley&#93;, K’naz &#91;Hunter&#93;, and Timna &#91;restrained&#93;, and ‘Amalek &#91;Dweller in a Valley&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> The sons of Re’u &#91;friend&#93;’el &#91;friend of God&#93;; Nachat &#91;rest&#93;, Zerach &#91;Rising&#93;, Shammah &#91;astonishment&#93;, and Mizah &#91;fear&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Genesis 36:20–30"> And the sons of Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;; Lotan &#91;covering&#93;, and Shoval &#91;flowing&#93;, and Tziv’on &#91;coloured&#93;, and ‘Anah &#91;answer&#93;, and Dishon &#91;thresher&#93;, and ‘Ezer &#91;treasure&#93;, and Dishan &#91;thresher&#93;.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Genesis 36:23–24"> The sons of Shoval &#91;flowing&#93;; ‘Alyan &#91;tall&#93;, and Manachat &#91;rest&#93;, and ‘Eival &#91;Stone&#93;, Sh’fi &#91;bold&#93;, and Onam &#91;vigorous&#93;. And the sons of Tziv’on &#91;coloured&#93;; Ayah &#91;falcon&#93;, and ‘Anah &#91;answer&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Genesis 36:25–26"> The sons of ‘Anah &#91;answer&#93;; Dishon &#91;thresher&#93;. And the sons of Dishon &#91;thresher&#93;; ‘Amram &#91;exalted people&#93;, and Eshban &#91;fire of discernment&#93;, and Yitran &#91;advantage&#93;, and K’ran &#91;lyre&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="Lamentations 4:21; Genesis 36:27–28"> The sons of ‘Ezer &#91;treasure&#93;; Bilhan &#91;their decrepitude&#93;, and Za’avan &#91;troubled&#93;, &#91;and&#93; Ya’akan &#91;let him oppress them&#93;. The sons of Dishan &#91;thresher&#93;; ‘Utz &#91;wooded&#93;, and Aran &#91;joyous&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43" data-xref="Genesis 36:31–43; Numbers 24:17–19; Genesis 49:10"> Now these &#91;are&#93; the kings that reigned in the land of Edom &#91;Red&#93; before &#91;any&#93; king reigned over the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Bela &#91;Destruction&#93; the son of B’or &#91;Burning&#93;: and the name of his city &#91;was&#93; Dinhavah &#91;give you judgement&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44" data-xref="Isaiah 34:6; Isaiah 63:1; Amos 1:12"> And when Bela &#91;Destruction&#93; was dead, Yovav &#91;A Desert&#93; the son of Zerach &#91;Rising&#93; of Botzrah &#91;sheepfold or fortress&#93; reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45" data-xref="Genesis 36:11; Job 2:11"> And when Yovav &#91;A Desert&#93; was dead, Husham &#91;haste&#93; of the land of the Temani &#91;southward&#93; reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> And when Husham &#91;haste&#93; was dead, Hadad &#91;mighty&#93; the son of B’dad &#91;solitary&#93;, which struck Midyan &#91;Strife&#93; in the field of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, reigned in his stead: and the name of his city &#91;was&#93; ‘Avit &#91;ruins&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47" data-xref="Genesis 36:36"> And when Hadad &#91;mighty&#93; was dead, Samlah &#91;garment&#93; of Masrekah &#91;vineyard of nable vines&#93; reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48" data-xref="Genesis 36:37"> And when Samlah &#91;garment&#93; was dead, Sha’ul &#91;Desired&#93; of Rehoboth by the river reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> And when Sha’ul &#91;Desired&#93; was dead, Ba’al-Hanan &#91;Ba’al the gracious&#93; the son of ‘Akhbor &#91;mouse&#93; reigned in his stead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="Genesis 36:39"> And when Ba’al-Hanan &#91;Ba’al the gracious&#93; was dead, Hadad &#91;mighty&#93; reigned in his stead: and the name of his city &#91;was&#93; Pa’i &#91;bleating&#93;; and his wife’s name &#91;was&#93; M’heitave’el &#91;favoured of God&#93;, the daughter of Matred &#91;pushing forward&#93;, the daughter of Mei-Zahav &#91;waters of gold&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51" data-xref="Genesis 36:40"> Hadad &#91;mighty&#93; died also. And the dukes of Edom &#91;Red&#93; were; chieftain Timnah &#91;Portion&#93;, chieftain ‘Alvah &#91;evil&#93;, chieftain Y’tet &#91;a nail&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> Chieftain Oholivamah &#91;tent of the high place&#93;, chieftain Elah &#91;an oak&#93;, chieftain Pinon &#91;darkness&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> Chieftain K’naz &#91;Hunter&#93;, chieftain Teman &#91;south&#93;, chieftain Mitzvar &#91;fortress&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54" data-xref="Genesis 36:41–43"> Chieftain Migdi’el &#91;prince of God&#93;, chieftain ‘Iram &#91;belonging to a city&#93;. These &#91;are&#93; the dukes of Edom &#91;Red&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 3:38; Genesis 4:25–32"> These &#91;are&#93; the sons of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93;, Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93;, Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;, and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;, and Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> Dan &#91;Judge&#93;, Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, and Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;, Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;, and Asher &#91;Happy&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Judah 1:14; Genesis 5:21–31; Luke 3:36–37"> The sons of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;; ‘Er &#91;awake&#93;, and Onan &#91;strong&#93;, and Shelah &#91;A Petition&#93;: &#91;which&#93; three were born unto him of the daughter of Bat-Shua &#91;daughter of wealth&#93; the Kena’anit &#91;zealous&#93;. And ‘Er &#91;awake&#93;, the firstborn of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, was evil in the sight of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and he killed him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Genesis 5:32; Genesis 9:18; Luke 17:26"> And Tamar &#91;palm-tree&#93; his daughter in law bore him Partzi &#91;Breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet)&#93; and Zerach &#91;Rising&#93;. All the sons of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; &#91;were&#93; five.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 10:1–5; Ezekiel 38:6; Ezekiel 27:13"> The sons of Partzi &#91;Breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet)&#93;; Hetzron &#91;surrounded by a wall&#93;, and Hamul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 10:3"> And the sons of Zerach &#91;Rising&#93;; Zimri &#91;My Music&#93;, and Eitan &#91;enduring&#93;, and Heiman &#91;faithful&#93;, and Kolkol &#91;sustaining&#93;, and Dara &#91;the arm&#93;: five of them in all.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Jeremiah 2:10; Numbers 24:24; Isaiah 66:19"> And the sons of Karmi &#91;Fruitful, Noble&#93;; ‘Akhar &#91;troubler&#93;, the troubler of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, who transgressed in the thing accursed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> And the sons of Eitan &#91;enduring&#93;; ‘Azaryah &#91;Yehovah has helped&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> The sons also of Hetzron &#91;surrounded by a wall&#93;, that were born unto him; Yerachme’el &#91;my God have pity&#93;, and Ram &#91;high or exalted&#93;, and K’luvai &#91;my caged one&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Genesis 10:8–12; Micah 5:6"> And Ram &#91;high or exalted&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) ‘Amminadav &#91;family of the prince&#93;; and ‘Amminadav &#91;family of the prince&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Nachshon &#91;Enchanter&#93;, prince of the children of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 10:13–18"> And Nachshon &#91;Enchanter&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Salma, and Salma begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Bo’az &#91;a high rate of movement&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Deuteronomy 2:23; Jeremiah 47:4; Amos 9:7"> And Bo’az &#91;a high rate of movement&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) ‘Oved &#91;serving&#93;, and ‘Oved &#91;serving&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Yishai &#91;I possess&#93;,</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 10:26–27"> And they were helped against them, and the Hagri’im &#91;wanderer&#93; were delivered into their hand, and all that &#91;were&#93; with them: for they cried to God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in the battle, and he was intreated of them; because they put their trust in him.</span>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> But Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons offered upon the altar of the burnt offering, and on the altar of incense, &#91;and were appointed&#93; for all the work of the &#91;place&#93; most holy, and to make an atonement for Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, according to all that Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; the servant of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; had commanded.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50" data-xref="Genesis 36:39"> And these &#91;are&#93; the sons of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;; El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; his son, Pinchas &#91;mouth of brass&#93; his son, Avishua &#91;my father is safety&#93; his son,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51" data-xref="Genesis 36:40"> Buki &#91;Wasting&#93; his son, ‘Uzi &#91;strong&#93; his son, Z’rachyah &#91;Yehovah has risen&#93; his son,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> M’rayot &#91;rebellious&#93; his son, Amaryah &#91;Yehovah speaks&#93; his son, Achituv &#91;my brother is good&#93; his son,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> Tzadok &#91;righteous&#93; his son, Achima’atz &#91;my brother is anger (wrath)&#93; his son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54" data-xref="Genesis 36:41–43"> Now these &#91;are&#93; their dwelling places throughout their castles in their coasts, of the sons of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, of the families of the K’hati: for theirs was the lot.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> And they gave them Hevron &#91;Seat of Association&#93; in the land of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and the suburbs thereof round about it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> But the fields of the city, and the villages thereof, they gave to Kalev &#91;Dog&#93; the son of Y’funeh &#91;He Will Face&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57"> And to the sons of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; they gave the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, &#91;namely&#93;, Hevron &#91;Seat of Association&#93;, &#91;the city&#93; of refuge, and Livnah &#91;Pavement&#93; with her suburbs, and Yatir &#91;Plenty&#93;, and Esht’moa &#91;I will make myself heard&#93;, with their suburbs,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v58" data-v="58"> And Hilen &#91;place of caves&#93; with her suburbs, D’vir &#91;Sanctuary&#93; with her suburbs,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v59" data-v="59"> And ‘Ashan &#91;Smoke&#93; with her suburbs, and Beit-Shemesh &#91;House of the Sun&#93; with her suburbs:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v60" data-v="60"> And out of the tribe of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;; Geva &#91;hill&#93; with her suburbs, and ‘Alemet &#91;covering&#93; with her suburbs, and ‘Anatot &#91;answers to prayer&#93; with her suburbs. All their cities throughout their families &#91;were&#93; thirteen cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v61" data-v="61"> And unto the sons of K’hat &#91;assembly, congregation&#93;, &#91;which were&#93; left of the family of that tribe, &#91;were cities given&#93; out of the half tribe, &#91;namely, out of&#93; the half &#91;tribe&#93; of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, by lot, ten cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v62" data-v="62"> And to the sons of Gershom &#91;Exiled One&#93; throughout their families out of the tribe of Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;, and out of the tribe of Asher &#91;Happy&#93;, and out of the tribe of Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;, and out of the tribe of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; in Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93;, thirteen cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v63" data-v="63"> Unto the sons of M’rari &#91;Bitter&#93; &#91;were given&#93; by lot, throughout their families, out of the tribe of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93;, and out of the tribe of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;, and out of the tribe of Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;, twelve cities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v64" data-v="64"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; gave to the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;these&#93; cities with their suburbs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v65" data-v="65"> And they gave by lot out of the tribe of the children of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and out of the tribe of the children of Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93;, and out of the tribe of the children of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, these cities, which are called by &#91;their&#93; names.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v66" data-v="66"> And &#91;the residue (part that remains, the rest)&#93; of the families of the sons of K’hat &#91;assembly, congregation&#93; had cities of their coasts out of the tribe of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v67" data-v="67"> And they gave unto them, &#91;of&#93; the cities of refuge, Sh’khem &#91;Shoulder&#93; in mount Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; with her suburbs; &#91;they gave&#93; also Gezer &#91;Portion&#93; with her suburbs,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v68" data-v="68"> And Yokme’am &#91;gathered by the people&#93; with her suburbs, and Beit-Horon &#91;House of Hollowness&#93; with her suburbs,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v69" data-v="69"> And Ayalon &#91;Field of Deer&#93; with her suburbs, and Gat-Rimmon &#91;winepress of the pomegranate&#93; with her suburbs:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v70" data-v="70"> And out of the half tribe of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;; ‘Aner &#91;boy&#93; with her suburbs, and Bil’am &#91;not of the people&#93; with her suburbs, for the family of the remnant of the sons of K’hat &#91;assembly, congregation&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v71" data-v="71"> Unto the sons of Gershom &#91;Exiled One&#93; &#91;were given&#93; out of the family of the half tribe of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, Golan &#91;Their Captivity&#93; in Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93; with her suburbs, and ‘Ashtarot &#91;Star (female deity)&#93; with her suburbs:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v72" data-v="72"> And out of the tribe of Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;; Kedesh &#91;Holy Place&#93; with her suburbs, Dovrat &#91;Word&#93; with her suburbs,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v73" data-v="73"> And Ramot &#91;Heights&#93; with her suburbs, and ‘Anem &#91;fountains&#93; with her suburbs:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v74" data-v="74"> And out of the tribe of Asher &#91;Happy&#93;; Mashal &#91;entreaty&#93; with her suburbs, and Avdon &#91;Servile&#93; with her suburbs,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v75" data-v="75"> And Hukok &#91;appointed&#93; with her suburbs, and Rechov &#91;Broad Place&#93; with her suburbs:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v76" data-v="76"> And out of the tribe of Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;; Kedesh &#91;Holy Place&#93; in Galil &#91;circuit, district&#93; with her suburbs, and Hamon &#91;warm springs&#93; with her suburbs, and Kiryatayim &#91;Two Cities&#93; with her suburbs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v77" data-v="77"> Unto the rest of the children of M’rari &#91;Bitter&#93; &#91;were given&#93; out of the tribe of Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;, Rimmon &#91;Pomegranate&#93; with her suburbs, Tavor &#91;Mound&#93; with her suburbs:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v78" data-v="78"> And on the other side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; by Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93;, on the east side of Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, &#91;were given them&#93; out of the tribe of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93;, Betzer &#91;Remote Fortress&#93; in the wilderness with her suburbs, and Yahatz &#91;trampled down&#93; with her suburbs,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v79" data-v="79"> K’demot &#91;Eastern&#93; also with her suburbs, and Mepha’et &#91;Spendour or Height&#93; with her suburbs:</span>

<span className="v" id="v80" data-v="80"> And out of the tribe of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;; Ramot &#91;Heights&#93; in Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93; with her suburbs, and Machanayim &#91;Two Camps&#93; with her suburbs,</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 3:38; Genesis 4:25–32"> Now the sons of Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93; &#91;were&#93;, Tola &#91;worm&#93;, and Puah &#91;Splendid&#93;, Yashuv &#91;He Will Return&#93;, and Shimron &#91;Watch-Height&#93;, four.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> And the sons of Tola &#91;worm&#93;; ‘Uzi &#91;strong&#93;, and Refayah &#91;healed of Yehovah&#93;, and Yeri’el &#91;taught by God&#93;, and Yachmai &#91;whom Yehovah guards&#93;, and Yivsam &#91;pleasant&#93;, and Sh’mu’el &#91;His Name is El&#93;, heads of their father’s house, &#91;that is to say&#93;, of Tola &#91;worm&#93;: &#91;they were&#93; valiant men of might in their generations; whose number &#91;was&#93; in the days of David &#91;beloved&#93; two and twenty thousand and six hundred.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Judah 1:14; Genesis 5:21–31; Luke 3:36–37"> And the sons of ‘Uzi &#91;strong&#93;; Yizrachyah &#91;Yehoveehah will shine&#93;: and the sons of Yizrachyah &#91;Yehoveehah will shine&#93;; Mikha’el &#91;Who is Like God?&#93;, and ‘Ovadyah &#91;servant of Yehovah&#93;, and Yo’el &#91;Yehovah is God&#93;, Yishiyah &#91;Yehovah will lend&#93;, five: all of them chief men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> And the sons of Bakher &#91;Young Camel&#93;; Z’mirah &#91;song&#93;, and Yo’ash &#91;Given by the Lord&#93;, and Eli’ezer &#91;God is help&#93;, and Elyo’eina &#91;unto Yehovah are my eyes&#93;, and ‘Omri &#91;pupil of Yehovah&#93;, and Yerimot &#91;He is Most High&#93;, and Aviyah &#91;Yehovah is (my) father&#93;, and ‘Anatot &#91;answers to prayer&#93;, and ‘Almet &#91;covering&#93;. All these &#91;are&#93; the sons of Bakher &#91;Young Camel&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> And the number of them, after their genealogy by their generations, heads of the house of their fathers, mighty men of valour, &#91;was&#93; twenty thousand and two hundred.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 10:13–18"> All these the sons of Y’dia’el &#91;God makes known&#93;, by the heads of their fathers, mighty men of valour, &#91;were&#93; seventeen thousand and two hundred &#91;soldiers&#93;, fit to go out for war &#91;and&#93; battle.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Genesis 11:12–15; Genesis 10:24"> And his sister Hammolekhet &#91;the queen&#93; bore Ish-Hod &#91;splendour&#93; &#91;man of majesty&#93;, and Avi-ezer &#91;My Father is Help&#93;, and Machlah &#91;disease&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Genesis 10:21; Genesis 11:16–17; Genesis 10:25"> And the sons of Sh’mida &#91;Wise&#93; were, Achyan &#91;brotherly&#93;, and Sh’khem &#91;Shoulder&#93;, and Lik’chi &#91;learning&#93;, and Ani’am &#91;I am the people, or lament of the people&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And Zavad &#91;he endows&#93; his son, and Shutelach &#91;Noise of Breaking&#93; his son, and ‘Ezer &#91;treasure&#93;, and El’ad &#91;God has testified&#93;, whom the men of Gat &#91;Winepress&#93; &#91;that were&#93; born in &#91;that&#93; land killed, because they came down to take away their cattle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Genesis 10:28"> And Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; their father mourned many days, and his brethren came to comfort him.</span>

<div className="bp">

In these dwelt the children of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93; the son of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Isaiah 21:11; Genesis 25:15"> The sons of Asher &#91;Happy&#93;; Yimnah &#91;right hand&#93;, and Yishvah &#91;he will resemble&#93;, and Yishvi &#91;he will resemble&#93;, and B’ri’i &#91;With a Friend&#93;, and Serach &#91;the prince breathed&#93; their sister.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And the sons of B’ri’i &#91;With a Friend&#93;; Hever &#91;Comrade&#93;, and Malki’el &#91;My King is God&#93;, who &#91;is&#93; the father of Birzayit &#91;in leanness, or choice olive&#93;.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Isaiah 60:6"> And the sons of Yaflet &#91;he will deliver&#93;; Pasakh &#91;cut off&#93;, and Bimhal &#91;in circumcision&#93;, and ‘Ashvat &#91;sleek&#93;. These &#91;are&#93; the children of Yaflet &#91;he will deliver&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Genesis 32:28; Acts 7:8; Gosel 1:2"> And the sons of Shamer &#91;preserved&#93;; Achi &#91;my brother&#93;, and Rohgah &#91;yelling (loud complaining)&#93;, Yakh’bah &#91;hidden&#93;, and Aram &#91;exalted&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Genesis 36:9–10; Genesis 36:4–5"> And the sons of his brother Helem &#91;smiter&#93;; Zofach &#91;a pot&#93;, and Yimnah &#91;He will restrain&#93;, and Shelesh &#91;might&#93;, and ‘Amal &#91;labour&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 10:26–27"> And Pinchas &#91;mouth of brass&#93; the son of El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; was the ruler over them in time past, &#91;and&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;was&#93; with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> &#91;And&#93; Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93; the son of Meshelemiah &#91;was&#93; porter of the door of the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Genesis 10:28"> All these &#91;which were&#93; chosen to be porters in the gates &#91;were&#93; two hundred and twelve. These were reckoned by their genealogy in their villages, whom David &#91;beloved&#93; and Sh’mu’el &#91;His name is El&#93; the seer did ordain in their set office.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="1 Kings 10:11; Genesis 25:18; 1 Samuel 15:7"> So they and their children &#91;had&#93; the oversight of the gates of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;namely&#93;, the house of the tabernacle, by wards.</span>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Luke 3:35"> And their brethren, &#91;which were&#93; in their villages, &#91;were&#93; to come after seven days from time to time with them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Luke 3:34–35"> For these Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, the four chief porters, were in &#91;their&#93; set office, and were over the chambers and treasuries of the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Genesis 11:27–32; Nehemiah 9:7; Joshua 24:2"> And they lodged round about the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, because the charge &#91;was&#93; upon them, and the opening thereof every morning &#91;pertained&#93; to them.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 17:19–21; Genesis 21:2–5; Genesis 21:9–10"> And &#91;certain&#93; of them had the charge of the ministering vessels, that they should bring them in and out by tale.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Isaiah 60:7; Genesis 25:12–16; Song of Solomon 1:5"> &#91;Some&#93; of them also &#91;were&#93; appointed to oversee the vessels, and all the instruments of the sanctuary, and the fine flour, and the wine, and the oil, and the frankincense, and the spices.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Isaiah 21:11; Genesis 25:15"> And &#91;some&#93; of the sons of the priests made the ointment of the spices.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And Mattityah &#91;gift of Yehovah&#93;, &#91;one&#93; of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, who &#91;was&#93; the firstborn of Shalum &#91;retribution&#93; the Korchi &#91;one of frost, ice, hail, bald&#93;, had the set office over the things that were made in the pans.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Genesis 25:1–4; Jeremiah 25:23; Judges 6:1–6"> And &#91;other&#93; of their brethren, of the sons of the K’hati, &#91;were&#93; over the showbread, to prepare &#91;it&#93; every Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Isaiah 60:6"> And these &#91;are&#93; the singers, chief of the fathers of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, &#91;who remaining&#93; in the chambers &#91;were&#93; free: for they were employed in &#91;that&#93; work day and night.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Genesis 32:28; Acts 7:8; Gosel 1:2"> These chief fathers of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;were&#93; chief throughout their generations; these dwelt at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Genesis 36:9–10; Genesis 36:4–5"> And in Giv’on &#91;Hill City&#93; dwelt the father of Giv’on &#91;Hill City&#93;, Jehiel, whose wife’s name &#91;was&#93; Ma’akhah &#91;oppression&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And G’dor &#91;Wall&#93;, and Achyo &#91;brotherly or fraternal&#93;, and Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93;, and Miklot &#91;rods (clubs)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Genesis 36:20–30"> And Miklot &#91;rods (clubs)&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Shim’am &#91;their fame&#93;. And they also dwelt with their brethren at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, over against their brethren.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Deuteronomy 2:12; Genesis 36:22; Deuteronomy 2:22"> And Ner &#91;lamp&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Kish &#91;bent&#93;; and Kish &#91;bent&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;; and Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93;, and Malkishua &#91;my king is wealth&#93;, and Avinadav &#91;my father is noble&#93;, and Eshba’al &#91;a man of lord&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Genesis 36:23–24"> And the son of Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93; &#91;was&#93; M’riv-Ba’al &#91;lord is my advocate&#93;: and M’riv-Ba’al &#91;lord is my advocate&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Mikhay’hu &#91;Who is Like God?&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Genesis 36:25–26"> And the sons of Mikhay’hu &#91;Who is Like God?&#93; &#91;were&#93;, Piton &#91;harmless&#93;, and Melekh &#91;king&#93;, and Tahrea, &#91;and Achaz &#91;he has grasped&#93;&#93;.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43" data-xref="Genesis 36:31–43; Numbers 24:17–19; Genesis 49:10"> And Motza &#91;fountain&#93; begat (to be father of; be ancestor of) Bin’a &#91;fountain&#93;; and Refayah &#91;healed of Yehovah&#93; his son, El’asah &#91;God has made&#93; his son, Atzel &#91;he has reserved&#93; his son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44" data-xref="Isaiah 34:6; Isaiah 63:1; Amos 1:12"> And Atzel &#91;he has reserved&#93; had six sons, whose names &#91;are&#93; these, ‘Azrikam &#91;help against the enemy&#93;, Bokhru &#91;firstborn&#93;, and Yishma’el &#91;God will hear&#93;, and Sh’aryah &#91;valued by Yehovah&#93;, and ‘Ovadyah &#91;servant of Yehovah&#93;, and Hanan &#91;he is merciful&#93;: these &#91;were&#93; the sons of Atzel &#91;he has reserved&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 3:38; Genesis 4:25–32"> Now the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; fought against Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; fled from before the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, and fell down slain in mount Gilboa &#91;swollen heap&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> And the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; followed hard after Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, and after his sons; and the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; killed Y’honatan &#91;Yehovah has Given&#93;, and Avinadav &#91;my father is noble&#93;, and Malkishua &#91;my king is wealth&#93;, the sons of Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Judah 1:14; Genesis 5:21–31; Luke 3:36–37"> And the battle went sore against Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, and the archers hit him, and he was wounded of the archers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Genesis 5:32; Genesis 9:18; Luke 17:26"> Then said Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; to his armourbearer, Draw youri sword, and thrust me through with that; lest these uncircumcised come and abuse me. But his armourbearer would not; for he was sore afraid. So Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; took a sword, and fell upon it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 10:1–5; Ezekiel 38:6; Ezekiel 27:13"> And when his armourbearer saw that Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; was dead, he fell likewise on the sword, and died.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 10:3"> So Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; died, and his three sons, and all his house died together.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Jeremiah 2:10; Numbers 24:24; Isaiah 66:19"> And when all the men of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that &#91;were&#93; in the valley saw that they fled, and that Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and his sons were dead, then they forsook their cities, and fled: and the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; came and dwelt in them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> And it came to pass on the morrow, when the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; came to strip the slain, that they found Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; and his sons fallen in mount Gilboa &#91;swollen heap&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> And when they had stripped him, they took his head, and his armour, and sent into the land of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; round about, to carry tidings unto their idols, and to the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Genesis 10:8–12; Micah 5:6"> And they put his armour in the house of their gods, and fastened his head in the temple of Dagon.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 10:13–18"> And when all Yavesh-Gil’ad &#91;Dry Hill of Testimony&#93; heard all that the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; had done to Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Deuteronomy 2:23; Jeremiah 47:4; Amos 9:7"> They arose, all the valiant men, and took away the body of Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;, and the bodies of his sons, and brought them to Yavesh &#91;dry&#93;, and buried their bones under the oak in Yavesh &#91;dry&#93;, and fasted seven days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Genesis 9:22; Genesis 23:20; Genesis 10:15–19"> So Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; died for his transgression which he committed against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;even&#93; against the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which he kept not, and also for asking &#91;counsel&#93; of &#91;one that had&#93; a familiar spirit, to enquire &#91;of it&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Genesis 15:21; Joshua 3:10; Exodus 33:2"> And enquired not of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): therefore he killed him, and turned the kingdom unto David &#91;beloved&#93; the son of Yishai &#91;I possess&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 3:38; Genesis 4:25–32"> Then all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; gathered themselves to David &#91;beloved&#93; unto Hevron &#91;Seat of Association&#93;, saying, Behold, we &#91;are&#93; youi bone and youi flesh.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> And moreover in time past, even when Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93; was king, youi &#91;were&#93; he that led out and brought in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; said unto youi, youi shall feed my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and youi shall be ruler over my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Judah 1:14; Genesis 5:21–31; Luke 3:36–37"> Therefore came all the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to the king to Hevron &#91;Seat of Association&#93;; and David &#91;beloved&#93; made a covenant with them in Hevron &#91;Seat of Association&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and they anointed David &#91;beloved&#93; king over Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, according to the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) by Sh’mu’el &#91;His name is El&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Genesis 5:32; Genesis 9:18; Luke 17:26"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; and all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; went to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; Y’vus &#91;Threshing Place&#93;; where the Y’vusi &#91;Threshers, Trampled Under Foot&#93; &#91;were&#93;, the inhabitants of the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 10:1–5; Ezekiel 38:6; Ezekiel 27:13"> And the inhabitants of Y’vus &#91;Threshing Place&#93; said to David &#91;beloved&#93;, youi shall not come here. Nevertheless David &#91;beloved&#93; took the castle of Tziyon &#91;parched place&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; the city of David &#91;beloved&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 10:3"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said, Whosoever smites the Y’vusi &#91;Threshers, Trampled Under Foot&#93; first shall be chief and captain. So Yo’av &#91;Yehovah is father&#93; the son of Tz’ruyah &#91;balsam&#93; went first up, and was chief.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Jeremiah 2:10; Numbers 24:24; Isaiah 66:19"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; dwelt in the castle; therefore they called it the city of David &#91;beloved&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> And he built the city round about, even from Millo &#91;fortification (defensive structure) or mound&#93; round about: and Yo’av &#91;Yehovah is father&#93; repaired the rest of the city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> So David &#91;beloved&#93; waxed greater and greater: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93; &#91;was&#93; with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Genesis 10:8–12; Micah 5:6"> These also &#91;are&#93; the chief of the mighty men whom David &#91;beloved&#93; had, who strengthened themselves with him in his kingdom, &#91;and&#93; with all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, to make him king, according to the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) concerning Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 10:13–18"> And this &#91;is&#93; the number of the mighty men whom David &#91;beloved&#93; had; Yashov’am &#91;the people will return&#93;, an Hakhmoni &#91;wise&#93;, the chief of the captains: he lifted up his spear against three hundred slain &#91;by him&#93; at one time.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Deuteronomy 2:23; Jeremiah 47:4; Amos 9:7"> And after him &#91;was&#93; El’azar &#91;God has helped&#93; the son of Dodo &#91;his beloved&#93;, the Achochi &#91;God prevails&#93;, who &#91;was one&#93; of the three mighties.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Genesis 9:22; Genesis 23:20; Genesis 10:15–19"> He was with David &#91;beloved&#93; at Pas-Damim &#91;boundary of blood&#93;, and there the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; were gathered together to battle, where was a parcel of ground full of barley; and the people fled from before the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Genesis 15:21; Joshua 3:10; Exodus 33:2"> And they set themselves in the midst of &#91;that&#93; parcel, and delivered it, and killed the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;; and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) saved &#91;them&#93; by a great deliverance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Exodus 3:8; 1 Kings 9:20; Exodus 13:5"> Now three of the thirty captains went down to the rock to David &#91;beloved&#93;, into the cave of ‘Adulam &#91;Justice of the People&#93;; and the host of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; encamped in the valley of Refa’im &#91;giants&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 34:8; 1 Kings 8:65"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; &#91;was&#93; then in the hold, and the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;’ Garrison (fortification; military post) &#91;was&#93; then at Beit-Lechem &#91;House of Bread&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Genesis 10:22–32; Ezra 4:2; Ezekiel 32:24"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; longed, and said, Oh that one would give me drink of the water of the well of Beit-Lechem &#91;House of Bread&#93;, that &#91;is&#93; at the gate!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Genesis 11:12–15; Genesis 10:24"> And the three broke through the host of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, and drew water out of the well of Beit-Lechem &#91;House of Bread&#93;, that &#91;was&#93; by the gate, and took &#91;it&#93;, and brought &#91;it&#93; to David &#91;beloved&#93;: but David &#91;beloved&#93; would not drink &#91;of&#93; it, but poured it out to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate),</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Genesis 10:21; Genesis 11:16–17; Genesis 10:25"> And said, My God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; forbid it me, that I should do this thing: shall I drink the blood of these men that have put their lives in jeopardy? for with &#91;the jeopardy of&#93; their lives they brought it. Therefore he would not drink it. These things did these three mightiest.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 10:26–27"> And Avishai &#91;my father is a gift&#93; the brother of Yo’av &#91;Yehovah is father&#93;, he was chief of the three: for lifting up his spear against three hundred, he killed &#91;them&#93;, and had a name among the three.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Of the three, he was more honourable than the two; for he was their captain: howbeit he attained not to the &#91;first&#93; three.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Genesis 10:28"> B’nayahu &#91;Yehovah has built&#93; the son of Y’hoyada &#91;Yehovah knows&#93;, the son of a valiant man of Kavtze’el &#91;God Gathers&#93;, who had done many acts; he killed two lion-like men of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;: also he went down and killed a lion in a pit in a snowy day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="1 Kings 10:11; Genesis 25:18; 1 Samuel 15:7"> And he killed an Mitzri &#91;Egyptian&#93;, a man of &#91;great&#93; stature, five cubits high; and in the Mitzri &#91;Egyptian&#93;’s hand &#91;was&#93; a spear like a weaver’s beam; and he went down to him with a staff, and plucked the spear out of the Mitzri &#91;Egyptian&#93;’s hand, and killed him with his own spear.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Genesis 11:10–26; Luke 3:34–36"> These &#91;things&#93; did B’nayahu &#91;Yehovah has built&#93; the son of Y’hoyada &#91;Yehovah knows&#93;, and had the name among the three mighties.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Luke 3:35"> Behold, he was honourable among the thirty, but attained not to the &#91;first&#93; three: and David &#91;beloved&#93; set him over his guard.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Luke 3:34–35"> Also the valiant men of the armies &#91;were&#93;, ‘Asah’el &#91;God-made&#93; the brother of Yo’av &#91;Yehovah is father&#93;, Elchanan &#91;God has been gracious&#93; the son of Dodo &#91;his beloved&#93; of Beit-Lechem &#91;House of Bread&#93;,</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said unto all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, If &#91;it seem&#93; good unto you, and &#91;that it be&#93; of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, let us send abroad unto our brethren every where, &#91;that are&#93; left in all the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and with them &#91;also&#93; to the priests and Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;which are&#93; in their cities &#91;and&#93; suburbs, that they may gather themselves unto us:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Judah 1:14; Genesis 5:21–31; Luke 3:36–37"> And let us bring again the ark of our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; to us: for we enquired not at it in the days of Sha’ul &#91;desired&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Genesis 5:32; Genesis 9:18; Luke 17:26"> And all the congregation said that they would do so: for the thing was right in the eyes of all the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 10:1–5; Ezekiel 38:6; Ezekiel 27:13"> So David &#91;beloved&#93; gathered all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; together, from Shihor of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; even unto the entering of Hamat &#91;fortress&#93;, to bring the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; from Kiryat-Ye’arim &#91;City of Forests&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 10:3"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; went up, and all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, to Ba’alah &#91;mistress&#93;, &#91;that is&#93;, to Kiryat-Ye’arim &#91;City of Forests&#93;, which &#91;belonged&#93; to Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, to bring up there the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that dwells &#91;between&#93; the k’vurim (cherubim), whose name is called &#91;on it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Jeremiah 2:10; Numbers 24:24; Isaiah 66:19"> And they carried the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in a new cart out of the house of Avinadav &#91;my father is noble&#93;: and ‘Uza &#91;strength&#93; and Achyo &#91;brotherly or fraternal&#93; drove the cart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; and all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; played before God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; with all &#91;their&#93; might, and with singing, and with harps, and with psalteries, and with timbrels, and with cymbals, and with trumpets.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> And when they came unto the threshingfloor of Kidon &#91;javelin&#93;, ‘Uza &#91;strength&#93; put forth his hand to hold the ark; for the oxen stumbled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Genesis 10:8–12; Micah 5:6"> And the anger of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was kindled against ‘Uza &#91;strength&#93;, and he struck him, because he put his hand to the ark: and there he died before God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 10:13–18"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; was displeased, because the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had made a breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet) upon ‘Uza &#91;strength&#93;: wherefore that place is called Peretz-’Uza &#91;breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet) of Uzza&#93; to this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Deuteronomy 2:23; Jeremiah 47:4; Amos 9:7"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; was afraid of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; that day, saying, How shall I bring the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;home&#93; to me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Genesis 9:22; Genesis 23:20; Genesis 10:15–19"> So David &#91;beloved&#93; brought not the ark &#91;home&#93; to himself to the city of David &#91;beloved&#93;, but carried it aside into the house of ‘Oved-Edom &#91;servant of Edom&#93; the Gitti &#91;belonging to Gat (winepress)&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Genesis 15:21; Joshua 3:10; Exodus 33:2"> And the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; remained with the family of ‘Oved-Edom &#91;servant of Edom&#93; in his house three months. And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the house of ‘Oved-Edom &#91;servant of Edom&#93;, and all that he had.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 3:38; Genesis 4:25–32"> Now Hiram &#91;noble&#93; king of Tzor (Tyre) &#91;a rock&#93; sent messengers to David &#91;beloved&#93;, and timber of cedars, with masons and carpenters, to build him an house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; perceived that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had confirmed him king over Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, for his kingdom was lifted up on high, because of his people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> And when the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; heard that David &#91;beloved&#93; was anointed king over all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, all the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; went up to seek David &#91;beloved&#93;. And David &#91;beloved&#93; heard &#91;of it&#93;, and went out against them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Genesis 10:8–12; Micah 5:6"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; enquired of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, saying, Shall I go up against the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;? and will youi deliver them into my hand? <div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 10:13–18"> So they came up to Ba’al-P’ratzim &#91;lord of the breaks&#93;; and David &#91;beloved&#93; struck them there. Then David &#91;beloved&#93; said, God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has broken in upon my enemies by my hand like the breaking forth of waters: therefore they called the name of that place Ba’al-P’ratzim &#91;lord of the breaks&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Deuteronomy 2:23; Jeremiah 47:4; Amos 9:7"> And when they had left their gods there, David &#91;beloved&#93; gave a commandment, and they were burned with fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Genesis 9:22; Genesis 23:20; Genesis 10:15–19"> And the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; yet again spread themselves abroad in the valley.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Genesis 15:21; Joshua 3:10; Exodus 33:2"> Therefore David &#91;beloved&#93; enquired again of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; and God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; said unto him, Go not up after them; turn away from them, and come upon them over against the mulberry trees.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Exodus 3:8; 1 Kings 9:20; Exodus 13:5"> And it shall be, when youi shall hear a sound of going in the tops of the mulberry trees, &#91;that&#93; then youi shall go out to battle: for God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; is gone forth before youi to strike the host of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Numbers 34:8; 1 Kings 8:65"> David &#91;beloved&#93; therefore did as God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; commanded him: and they struck the host of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; from Giv’on &#91;Hill City&#93; even to Gezer &#91;portion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Genesis 10:22–32; Ezra 4:2; Ezekiel 32:24"> And the fame of David &#91;beloved&#93; went out into all lands; and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought the fear of him upon all nations.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> Then David &#91;beloved&#93; said, None ought to carry the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; but the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;: for them has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) chosen to carry the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and to minister unto him forever.</span>

<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Genesis 15:21; Joshua 3:10; Exodus 33:2"> So the priests and the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; sanctified themselves to bring up the ark of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Genesis 11:12–15; Genesis 10:24"> And with them their brethren of the second &#91;degree&#93;, Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93;, Ben &#91;son&#93;, and Ya’azi’el &#91;made bold by God&#93;, and Sh’miramot &#91;name of heights&#93;, and Jehiel, and ‘Uni &#91;afflicted&#93;, ‘Eli’av &#91;My Father is God&#93;, and B’nayahu &#91;Yehovah has built&#93;, and Ma’aseiyah &#91;work of Yehovah&#93;, and Mattityah &#91;gift of Yehovah&#93;, and Elif’lehu &#91;my God sets him apart&#93;, and Mikneyahu &#91;possession of Yehovah&#93;, and ‘Oved-Edom &#91;servant of Edom&#93;, and Ye’i’el &#91;God sweeps away&#93;, the porters.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Genesis 10:28"> And K’nanyaho &#91;Yehovah establishes&#93;, chief of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, &#91;was&#93; for song: he instructed about the song, because he &#91;was&#93; skilful.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Genesis 11:10–26; Luke 3:34–36"> And Sh’vanyah &#91;increased by Yehovah&#93;, and Y’hoshafat &#91;Yehovah has judged&#93;, and N’tan’el &#91;God’s Gift&#93;, and ‘Amasai &#91;burdensome&#93;, and Z’kharyah &#91;Yehovah remembers&#93;, and B’nayahu &#91;Yehovah has built&#93;, and Eli’ezer &#91;God is help&#93;, the priests, did blow with the trumpets before the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: and ‘Oved-Edom &#91;servant of Edom&#93; and Yechiyah &#91;Yehovah lives&#93; &#91;were&#93; doorkeepers for the ark.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Luke 3:34–35"> And it came to pass, when God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; helped the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; that bore the ark of the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), that they offered seven bullocks and seven rams.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 17:19–21; Genesis 21:2–5; Genesis 21:9–10"> Thus all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; brought up the ark of the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) with shouting, and with sound of the cornet (a wind instrument; horn; trumpet), and with trumpets, and with cymbals, making a noise with psalteries and harps.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 3:38; Genesis 4:25–32"> So they brought the ark of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and set it in the midst of the tent that David &#91;beloved&#93; had pitched for it: and they offered burnt sacrifices and peace offerings before God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> And when David &#91;beloved&#93; had made an end of offering the burnt offerings and the peace offerings, he Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the people in the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Judah 1:14; Genesis 5:21–31; Luke 3:36–37"> And he dealt to every one of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, both man and woman, to every one a loaf of bread, and a good piece of flesh, and raisin cakes.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Genesis 10:22–32; Ezra 4:2; Ezekiel 32:24"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said unto God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;Is it&#93; not I &#91;that&#93; commanded the people to be numbered? even I it is that have sinned and done evil indeed; but &#91;as for&#93; these sheep, what have they done? let youi hand, I pray youi, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, be on me, and on my father’s house; but not on youi people, that they should be plagued.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Genesis 11:12–15; Genesis 10:24"> Then the angel of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; to say to David &#91;beloved&#93;, that David &#91;beloved&#93; should go up, and set up an altar unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the threshingfloor of Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93; the Y’vusi &#91;Threshers&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Genesis 10:21; Genesis 11:16–17; Genesis 10:25"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; went up at the saying of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;, which he spoke in the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 10:26–27"> And Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93; turned back, and saw the angel; and his four sons with him hid themselves. Now Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93; was threshing wheat.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And as David &#91;beloved&#93; came to Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93;, Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93; looked and saw David &#91;beloved&#93;, and went out of the threshingfloor, and bowed himself to David &#91;beloved&#93; with &#91;his&#93; face to the ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Genesis 10:28"> Then David &#91;beloved&#93; said to Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93;, Grant me the place of &#91;this&#93; threshingfloor, that I may build an altar in it unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): youi shall grant it me for the full price: that the plague may be detained (held) from the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="1 Kings 10:11; Genesis 25:18; 1 Samuel 15:7"> And Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93; said unto David &#91;beloved&#93;, Take &#91;it&#93; to youi, and let my lord the king do &#91;that which is&#93; good in his eyes: lo, I give &#91;youi&#93; the oxen &#91;also&#93; for burnt offerings, and the threshing instruments for wood, and the wheat for the meat offering; I give it all.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Genesis 11:10–26; Luke 3:34–36"> And king David &#91;beloved&#93; said to Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93;, No; but I will truly buy it for the full price: for I will not take &#91;that&#93; which &#91;is&#93; youi for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), nor offer burnt offerings without cost.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Luke 3:35"> So David &#91;beloved&#93; gave to Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93; for the place six hundred shekels of gold by weight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Luke 3:34–35"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; built there an altar unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and offered burnt offerings and peace offerings, and called upon the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); and he answered him from heaven by fire upon the altar of burnt offering.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Genesis 17:19–21; Genesis 21:2–5; Genesis 21:9–10"> At that time when David &#91;beloved&#93; saw that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had answered him in the threshingfloor of Ornan &#91;light was perpetuated&#93; the Y’vusi &#91;Threshers&#93;, then he sacrificed there.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29" data-xref="Isaiah 60:7; Genesis 25:12–16; Song of Solomon 1:5"> For the tabernacle of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; made in the wilderness, and the altar of the burnt offering, &#91;were&#93; at that season in the high place at Giv’on &#91;Hill City&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Isaiah 21:11; Genesis 25:15"> But David &#91;beloved&#93; could not go before it to enquire of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: for he was afraid because of the sword of the angel of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Luke 3:38; Genesis 4:25–32"> Then David &#91;beloved&#93; said, This &#91;is&#93; the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and this &#91;is&#93; the altar of the burnt offering for Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; commanded to gather together the strangers that &#91;were&#93; in the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and he set masons to hew wrought stones to build the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 10:1–5; Ezekiel 38:6; Ezekiel 27:13"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said, Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; my son &#91;is&#93; young and tender, and the house &#91;that is&#93; to be built for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;must be&#93; exceeding magnificent, of fame and of glory throughout all countries: I will &#91;therefore&#93; now make preparation for it. So David &#91;beloved&#93; prepared abundantly before his death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 10:3"> Then he called for Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; his son, and charged him to build an house for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Jeremiah 2:10; Numbers 24:24; Isaiah 66:19"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said to Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, My son, as for me, it was in my mind to build an house unto the name of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> But the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came to me, saying, youi have shed blood abundantly, and have made great wars: youi shall not build an house unto my name, because youi have shed much blood upon the earth in my sight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Behold, a son shall be born to youi, who shall be a man of rest; and I will give him rest from all his enemies round about: for his name shall be Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93;, and I will give peace and quietness unto Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in his days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 10:13–18"> Now, my son, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) be with youi; and prosper youi, and build the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, as he has said of youi.</span>

<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Deuteronomy 2:23; Jeremiah 47:4; Amos 9:7"> Only the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) give youi wisdom and understanding, and give youi charge concerning Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that youi may keep the law of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Genesis 9:22; Genesis 23:20; Genesis 10:15–19"> Then shall youi prosper, if youi take heed to fulfil the statutes and judgments which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) charged Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; with concerning Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: be strong, and of good courage; dread not, nor be dismayed.</span>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Genesis 11:12–15; Genesis 10:24"> &#91;Is&#93; not the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; with you? and has he &#91;not&#93; given you rest on every side? for he has given the inhabitants of the land into my hand; and the land is subdued before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and before his people.</span>

<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Isaiah 21:11; Genesis 25:15"> And to stand every morning to thank and praise the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and likewise at even;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And to offer all burnt sacrifices unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the Shabbat, in the new moons, and on the set feasts, by number, according to the order commanded unto them, continually before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Luke 3:37; Genesis 5:12–20; Genesis 5:9"> Then David &#91;beloved&#93; the king stood up upon his feet, and said, Hear me, my brethren, and my people: &#91;As for me&#93;, I &#91;had&#93; in my heart to build an house of rest for the ark of the covenant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and for the footstool of our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and had made ready for the building:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Genesis 10:1–5; Ezekiel 38:6; Ezekiel 27:13"> And of all my sons, (for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has given me many sons,) he has chosen Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; my son to sit upon the throne of the kingdom of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) over Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 10:6–7"> Now therefore in the sight of all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; the congregation of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and in the audience of our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, keep and seek for all the commandments of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: that you2f may possess this good land, and leave &#91;it&#93; for an inheritance for your children after you forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> And youi, Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; my son, know youi the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of youri father, and serve him with a perfect heart and with a willing mind: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) searcheth all hearts, and understands all the imaginations of the thoughts: if youi seek him, he will be found of youi; but if youi forsake him, he will cast youi off forever.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Genesis 10:21; Genesis 11:16–17; Genesis 10:25"> All &#91;this, said David &#91;beloved&#93;&#93;, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) made me understand in writing by &#91;his&#93; hand upon me, &#91;even&#93; all the works of this pattern.</span>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Genesis 10:26–27"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; said to Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;peace&#93; his son, Be strong and of good courage, and do &#91;it&#93;: fear not, nor be dismayed: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;even&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;will be&#93; with youi; he will not fail youi, nor forsake youi, until youi have finished all the work for the service of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Genesis 10:8–12; Micah 5:6"> For what reason David &#91;beloved&#93; Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) before all the congregation: and David &#91;beloved&#93; said, Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; youi, LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; our father, forever and ever.</span>
